{"forms":[{"form":"'n","tags":["alternative","nonstandard"]},{"form":"thanne","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"then","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"conj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The movie has a different name than I thought.","type":"example"},{"text":"she's taller than I am; she found his advice more witty than helpful; we have less work today than we had yesterday; We had no (other) choice than to return home; I love you more than (what) she does, but less than he, or than I do your sisters.","type":"example"},{"text":"Profits were higher than forecast.","type":"example"},{"text":"City dwellers have a higher cancer rate than do countrymen. Apparently, they drink more than is good for 'em.","type":"example"},{"text":"A lot bigger than I ever believe could be possible.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used in comparisons, to introduce the basis of comparison."]},{"glosses":["Because; for."],"raw_tags":["used with for"],"tags":["dialectal","obsolete","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"thăn"},{"enpr":"thĕn"},{"ipa":"/ðæn/"},{"ipa":"/ðɛn/"},{"enpr":"thən","tags":["unstressed"]},{"ipa":"/ðən/","tags":["unstressed"]},{"ipa":"[ðn̩]","tags":["unstressed"]}],"upstream_line":63945,"upstream_offset":381884899,"upstream_record_sha256":"0b33778c2d82f1cbf3c68913f654aa4701ef801cc54b967dc78d1349a11dc9f9","word":"than"}
